2 · Baseline müqayisəsi

⚠️ BASELINE YOXDUR — REQRESSİYA YOXLANILMADI. Bu səhifədəki rəqəmlər MÜTLƏQ dəyərlərdir: hansı case-in bu qaçışda SINDIĞI bilinmir, çünki müqayisə üçün əvvəlki snapshot verilməyib. «Reqressiya görünmür» ilə «reqressiya yoxdur» eyni şey deyil.

4 · Judge kalibrasiyası

Bu qaçışda LLM-as-judge grader-i İŞLƏDİLMƏYİB.
Bütün verdiktlər determinist grader-lərdən gəlir (regex, ifadə, tool çağırışı, retrieval) — yəni hesabatın heç bir rəqəmi model rəyindən asılı deyil və oflayn yenidən hesablana bilər. Judge case-ləri əlavə olunarsa bu bölmə uyğunluq, Cohen's κ və n rəqəmlərini göstərməyə borcludur.

5 · Kateqoriya üzrə keçmə dərəcəsi

Bir case bir neçə taqda görünə bilər — sətir cəmləri case sayından böyük ola bilər. «keçmə» məxrəci yalnız qiymətləndirilən (skip olmayan) case-lərdir.

giriş (tam)
An Aurora-branded product was delivered to me 25 months ago, in mid-2026, and it has failed. Is it still under warranty?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
Aurora-branded products carry a **24-month** warranty (30 months if you had Aurora Plus active at the time of purchase), so at 25 months out you'd only still be covered if you had an active Aurora Plus membership when you bought it.

Could you tell me:
1. Your order ID (so I can check the delivery/product details), and
2. Whether you had Aurora Plus at the time of purchase?

That will let me confirm whether your item is still in warranty.

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.7757212519645691 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[abde77a8-e2c8-4d0d-9414-688ec306a1b8] score=0.6861210465431213 doc=warranty-policy.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.0)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to products delivered between 2025-01-01 and 2026-01-31.

**A.1 Aurora-branded warranty (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 Aurora-branded products carried an **18-month** warranty. This was raised to 24 months in v3.1.

**A.2 Aurora Plus extension (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 there was **no** membership warranty extension.

**A.3 Diagnostic fee (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee was **15.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v3.0 → v3.1 approved by After-Sales Service on 2025-12-20, effective 2026-02-01.*

[e590190d-e833-47b5-b572-d502523f2789] score=0.6167099475860596 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

[f075e64d-ae2b-4980-8851-39f147473e4f] score=0.6461177468299866 doc=payments-and-billing.md
2. Cash on delivery
2.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, the COD handling fee, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

2.1 COD is available only for orders delivered inside Azerbaijan.

2.2 COD is available for orders with a total of **500.00 AZN or less**.

| Order total | COD available? |
|---|---|
| 499.99 AZN | Yes |
| 500.00 AZN | Yes |
| 500.01 AZN | No |

2.3 Orders above the COD limit must be paid by card, bank transfer, or wallet before dispatch.

2.4 COD orders carry a handling fee of **3.00 AZN**.

---

[c0a144df-a50a-40c8-a127-10372453edd1] score=0.5804330110549927 doc=international-shipping.md
3. Weight, size and value limits
3.1 The maximum shipping weight for a single international parcel is **30.0 kg**. A parcel weighing exactly 30.0 kg is accepted; a parcel above 30.0 kg is not accepted and the order is split or cancelled.

3.2 The maximum declared value for a single international parcel is **5,000.00 AZN**.

3.3 Cash on delivery is not available on any international order; see `payments-and-billing.md` §2.1.

---

[b4843b8a-b8e7-4a27-8801-585a6ef9f587] score=0.5726474523544312 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
5. Signature and high-value orders
5.1 Orders with a paid total **above 750.00 AZN** require an adult signature on delivery and cannot be left with a neighbour or at an unattended address.

5.2 Orders at or below 750.00 AZN may be left at the address without signature if the customer has enabled that option in their account.

---

**A.2 Instalment minimum (superseded 2026-05-01).** Under v1.8 the instalment minimum was **150.00 AZN** and only 3- and 6-month plans were offered.

**A.3 Store credit bonus (superseded 2026-05-01).** Under v1.8 store credit carried **no bonus**.

7. Address changes
7.1 A delivery address can be changed free of charge until the order is dispatched.

[762ead9a-46e4-4fc2-9f33-3716ee502458] score=0.5166535377502441 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
3. Delivery zones and service levels
3.1 Delivery estimates are counted in **business days from dispatch**, not from order placement.

| Zone | Coverage | Standard delivery | Express delivery |
|---|---|---|---|
| Zone 1 | Baku and Absheron peninsula | 1–2 business days | next business day |
| Zone 2 | Ganja, Sumgait, Mingachevir, Shirvan, Lankaran, Sheki, Nakhchivan city | 2–4 business days | 1–2 business days |
| Zone 3 | all other settlements | 4–7 business days | not available |

3.2 Delivery estimates are estimates, not guarantees. A parcel is treated as **delayed** only once it exceeds the upper bound of its zone estimate by **3 business days**.

3.3 Express delivery is available for orders confirmed before the cut-off in §2.1 and is not available to Zone 3.

---

[73cb1de6-76b1-4f0e-869f-53cb1f24fad0] score=0.5125133395195007 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
4. Shipping charges
4.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, express surcharges, heavy-item surcharges, COD handling fees, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

4.1 Standard shipping is **free** on orders with a paid total of **100.00 AZN or more**.

4.2 Orders with a paid total **below 100.00 AZN** are charged a flat standard shipping fee of **5.90 AZN**.

4.3 Express delivery costs **12.00 AZN** in Zone 1 and **19.00 AZN** in Zone 2, and this charge applies regardless of order value. The free shipping threshold in §4.1 applies only to standard shipping.

4.4 Parcels with a shipping weight **above 30.0 kg** carry a heavy-item surcharge of **25.00 AZN**. Parcels weighing exactly 30.0 kg are not surcharged.

[0820fc7e-4926-4475-81c9-44d1df7a2ba8] score=0.5906200408935547 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5. Price match
5.1 Aurora Goods matches a lower advertised price for an identical item, in stock, sold and delivered by a competitor registered in Azerbaijan.

5.2 A price match claim must be made within **14 calendar days** of the order date. Note that this period runs from the **order date**, not the delivery date, and is therefore counted differently from every return window in this corpus.

5.3 The maximum amount refunded under a single price match claim is **200.00 AZN**.

5.4 Price match is not available on:
- clearance and Final Sale items;
- items bought with a promotional code;
- marketplace, auction, second-hand, and grey-import listings;
- competitor prices that require a membership, app install, or bundle purchase to obtain;
- items where the competitor's total price including delivery is not lower than the Aurora Goods total price including delivery.

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.5297345519065857 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[c0a09907-1e58-4dcc-892d-fccf8d967702] score=0.5086363554000854 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
3. Promotional code rules
3.1 **One promotional code per order.** Codes cannot be combined with one another.

3.2 A promotional code cannot be applied after an order has been submitted, and an order cannot be cancelled and re-placed solely to apply a code.

3.3 Percentage codes apply to merchandise value only, never to shipping charges, COD handling fees, or the Aurora Plus membership fee.

3.4 Minimum spend stated in a campaign is measured **before** shipping charges and **after** any other reduction already reflected in the item price.

3.5 Store credit and gift cards are payment methods, not promotional codes, and may be used together with one promotional code.

---

[325f5cea-5346-4a83-99ff-746960974a6d] score=0.5040595531463623 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v5.1)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to orders placed between 2026-01-01 and 2026-05-31.

**A.1 Promotional return window (superseded 2026-06-01).** Under v5.1 promotional items carried a **10-day** return window.

**A.2 Promotional threshold (superseded 2026-06-01).** Under v5.1 an item counted as promotional at a discount of **25% or more**.

**A.3 Price match cap (superseded 2026-06-01).** Under v5.1 the price match cap was **100.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v5.1 → v5.2 approved by Commercial on 2026-05-12, effective 2026-06-01.*

[256e1f18-677c-4d33-bed8-bea197b506ea] score=0.47597646713256836 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
1. When an item counts as promotional
1.1 An item is **promotional** if any of the following is true at the moment of purchase:

- it was sold at a discount of **30% or more** off its list price;
- a promotional code was applied to it;
- it was part of a named seasonal campaign (Novruz, Back to School, Black Friday, Year End);
- it was a bundle component sold below its individual list price.

| Discount off list price | Promotional? |
|---|---|
| 29% | No — standard rules apply |
| 30% | Yes |
| 31% | Yes |

1.2 A discount of less than 30% applied without a promotional code and outside a named campaign does **not** make an item promotional. Ordinary markdowns and price reductions are not campaigns.

[9bf37918-619f-4d4f-b1d1-b3fcbaad8fbd] score=0.7835909128189087 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
4.5 Aurora Plus members receive free standard shipping with no minimum order value. See `account-and-membership.md` §4.

---

[8fe856c0-891b-47b6-b9b3-748fff04a718] score=0.6359598636627197 doc=account-and-membership.md
2. Aurora Plus membership
2.1 Aurora Plus is an annual paid membership costing **49.00 AZN per year**, charged in advance.

2.2 New members receive a **30-day free trial**. The membership fee is charged on the day after the trial ends unless the trial is cancelled before then.

2.3 One membership covers one account. Membership benefits cannot be shared with other accounts or transferred to another person.

2.4 Membership is "active" for the purpose of any benefit in this document if it was active on the date the relevant order was placed.

---

[741cbac0-8dd2-45e9-b1ca-f15b2a2f2a39] score=0.6091378927230835 doc=account-and-membership.md
Account and Aurora Plus Membership Policy
> **Document ID:** AG-POL-ACC
> **Version:** 2.0
> **Effective from:** 2026-03-01
> **Supersedes:** v1.7 (in force 2024-10-01 through 2026-02-28)
> **Scope:** all Aurora Goods customer accounts
> **Owner:** Customer Experience
> **Last reviewed:** 2026-06-30
> **Related documents:** `privacy-and-data.md`, `returns-and-refunds.md`, `shipping-and-delivery.md`, `warranty-policy.md`, `payments-and-billing.md`

---

[2031f6ab-6c4f-4f9b-b5aa-8ee8b75a8d89] score=0.6048288345336914 doc=account-and-membership.md
5. Cancelling Aurora Plus
5.1 A membership cancelled within **14 calendar days** of the charge date is refunded in full, provided no member benefit has been used in that period. Placing an order that used free member shipping counts as using a benefit.

5.2 A membership cancelled after the 14-day period is refunded **pro rata for each whole unused month remaining**, calculated from the cancellation date. Partial months are not refunded.

5.3 Benefits end on the cancellation date, not at the end of the paid year. A warranty extension already earned under §4.2 is not withdrawn.

5.4 A membership cancelled and re-taken within **12 months** is not eligible for a further free trial.

---

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.5921105742454529 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[510e0432-e9e3-43d3-8a33-91eb4650bd19] score=0.583008885383606 doc=international-shipping.md
4. Duties, taxes and shipping charges
4.0 **How order value is measured.** The threshold in §4.2 is measured on **merchandise value**, excluding international shipping charges and any duties.

4.1 International shipping is charged at checkout and is **never free**. There is no international equivalent of the domestic 100.00 AZN free-shipping threshold, and Aurora Plus membership does not provide free international shipping.

4.2 Orders with a merchandise value of **1,000.00 AZN or more** are shipped **DDP**: Aurora Goods prepays destination duties and import taxes, and the customer pays nothing further on delivery.

4.3 Orders below 1,000.00 AZN are shipped **DDU**: the customer is responsible for destination duties, import taxes, and any customs broker fee. These are charged by the destination authority, not by Aurora Goods.

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.5775320529937744 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[73cb1de6-76b1-4f0e-869f-53cb1f24fad0] score=0.5749751925468445 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
4. Shipping charges
4.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, express surcharges, heavy-item surcharges, COD handling fees, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

4.1 Standard shipping is **free** on orders with a paid total of **100.00 AZN or more**.

4.2 Orders with a paid total **below 100.00 AZN** are charged a flat standard shipping fee of **5.90 AZN**.

4.3 Express delivery costs **12.00 AZN** in Zone 1 and **19.00 AZN** in Zone 2, and this charge applies regardless of order value. The free shipping threshold in §4.1 applies only to standard shipping.

4.4 Parcels with a shipping weight **above 30.0 kg** carry a heavy-item surcharge of **25.00 AZN**. Parcels weighing exactly 30.0 kg are not surcharged.

giriş (tam)
We are a company. What is the minimum quantity for a business discount and how do we get a VAT invoice for a bulk order?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
That search didn't return anything about a business/B2B discount or VAT invoicing. Let me search more specifically.I checked the Aurora Goods knowledge base and there is no bulk/business (B2B) discount program and no minimum-quantity threshold defined anywhere in our policies. Here's what is actually documented:

- **No B2B/bulk discount tier exists.** All discounts come from promotions/campaigns (e.g., codes, seasonal campaigns) — there's no separate "business" pricing structure.
- **Invoicing:** Every order automatically gets an electronic receipt in the customer's order history, which is downloadable for 5 years. This receipt includes the order amount, VAT, and payment reference — but I don't see any documented process for requesting a separate formal VAT invoice with your company's tax details for a bulk purchase.

Since this falls outside what our knowledge base covers, I'll escalate this to a human agent who can advise on business ordering options and proper VAT invoicing for your company.I've opened ticket **ESC-20260901-0001** for this — here's the summary:

- **Business/bulk discount:** Our published policies don't define a minimum-quantity threshold for a business discount — there's no separate B2B pricing program in the knowledge base. This needs input from our Commercial team.
- **VAT invoice for bulk orders:** Standard orders automatically generate an electronic receipt (with VAT shown) in your order history, downloadable for 5 years. However, there's no documented process for a formal company VAT invoice request, so I've escalated this to Finance as well.

[f075e64d-ae2b-4980-8851-39f147473e4f] score=0.47029805183410645 doc=payments-and-billing.md
2. Cash on delivery
2.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, the COD handling fee, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

2.1 COD is available only for orders delivered inside Azerbaijan.

2.2 COD is available for orders with a total of **500.00 AZN or less**.

| Order total | COD available? |
|---|---|
| 499.99 AZN | Yes |
| 500.00 AZN | Yes |
| 500.01 AZN | No |

2.3 Orders above the COD limit must be paid by card, bank transfer, or wallet before dispatch.

2.4 COD orders carry a handling fee of **3.00 AZN**.

[c0a09907-1e58-4dcc-892d-fccf8d967702] score=0.44894033670425415 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
3. Promotional code rules
3.1 **One promotional code per order.** Codes cannot be combined with one another.

3.2 A promotional code cannot be applied after an order has been submitted, and an order cannot be cancelled and re-placed solely to apply a code.

3.3 Percentage codes apply to merchandise value only, never to shipping charges, COD handling fees, or the Aurora Plus membership fee.

3.4 Minimum spend stated in a campaign is measured **before** shipping charges and **after** any other reduction already reflected in the item price.

3.5 Store credit and gift cards are payment methods, not promotional codes, and may be used together with one promotional code.

[256e1f18-677c-4d33-bed8-bea197b506ea] score=0.4422064423561096 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
1. When an item counts as promotional
1.1 An item is **promotional** if any of the following is true at the moment of purchase:

- it was sold at a discount of **30% or more** off its list price;
- a promotional code was applied to it;
- it was part of a named seasonal campaign (Novruz, Back to School, Black Friday, Year End);
- it was a bundle component sold below its individual list price.

| Discount off list price | Promotional? |
|---|---|
| 29% | No — standard rules apply |
| 30% | Yes |
| 31% | Yes |

1.2 A discount of less than 30% applied without a promotional code and outside a named campaign does **not** make an item promotional. Ordinary markdowns and price reductions are not campaigns.

[17ba5db6-fb88-4626-957b-cf3aac682f0c] score=0.4884610176086426 doc=privacy-and-data.md
4. What erasure does not remove
4.1 Aurora Goods is required to retain transaction records for tax and accounting purposes. Erasure therefore does not remove the financial record of an order: order number, date, amount, VAT, and payment method reference are retained in the accounting ledger.

4.2 Retained ledger records are separated from the customer profile and are not used for marketing, personalisation, or support.

4.3 This retention obligation overrides an erasure request. A customer cannot require deletion of an accounting record inside the retention period.

1. Account creation and access

1.2 After **5 consecutive failed sign-in attempts** the account is locked for **30 minutes**. The counter resets after a successful sign-in or after the lock expires.

1.3 A password reset link is valid for **24 hours** and can be used once.

1.4 Two-factor authentication by SMS or authenticator app is optional and can be enabled at any time.

gözlənilən çağırış(lar) yoxdur və ya arqumenti uyğunsuzdur: [{'name': 'escalate_to_human'}]
giriş (tam)
I ordered the wrong size. Can I exchange it for a larger one instead of returning it?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
I can help with that. Could you give me your order ID (format ORD-#####) and the SKU of the item you'd like to exchange?

That said, I should mention upfront: let me check our policy on exchanges to give you accurate info.Aurora Goods doesn't have a direct "exchange" mechanism — there's no swap option in the system. To get a different size, the process is:

1. Return the original item for a refund (subject to the normal return window/eligibility rules — typically 14 days from delivery, or a different window if the item was promotional, international, or you're an Aurora Plus member).
2. Place a new order for the size you want.

If you give me your order ID and the SKU, I can check your specific return eligibility (window, any restocking fee, who pays return shipping) so you know exactly what to expect before you return it.

[1259e653-711d-4230-bb48-72bb926a7a98] score=0.4871954917907715 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8.2 Two consequences of this ladder are stated explicitly because they are frequently misread:
- An Aurora Plus member who buys a promotional item gets the **promotional** window, not the member window.
- An Aurora Plus member whose parcel is delivered outside Azerbaijan gets the **international** window, not the member window.

---

[91f9e5e0-4b95-44a2-a81e-1b43feffefd7] score=0.48696768283843994 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1.5 A return is a request to send goods back for a refund. A warranty claim is a request to repair or replace a defective product after the return window has closed. Warranty claims are governed by `warranty-policy.md`.

---

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.4775732755661011 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[836c7e8c-bec2-4e46-a640-6515116878ec] score=0.47616803646087646 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5.5 A successful price match is paid as store credit within **24 hours**, or to the original payment method within the timescales in `payments-and-billing.md` §6.2 if the customer requests it.

5.6 A price match does not make an item promotional and does not shorten its return window.

---

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.47236698865890503 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[41360bad-f3c0-4e2c-b858-ebbae9219738] score=0.47151362895965576 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6.4 Refunds for returned goods are approved only after warehouse inspection; see `returns-and-refunds.md` §7.2.

6.5 Where a COD order is refunded, the customer must supply an IBAN. Aurora Goods does not refund COD orders in cash.

---

[ab7a582e-fc44-4bc2-b79e-4ffc35c8988e] score=0.46826171875 doc=international-shipping.md
| Merchandise value | Terms | Who pays duties |
|---|---|---|
| 999.99 AZN | DDU | customer |
| 1,000.00 AZN | DDP | Aurora Goods |
| 1,000.01 AZN | DDP | Aurora Goods |

4.4 A parcel refused at customs, or abandoned because the customer declines to pay duties under §4.3, is treated as a refused delivery. Outbound shipping, duties already incurred, and return-to-origin freight are deducted from any refund.

---

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.466602623462677 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[bd129aaa-6a0d-4fd8-8717-d173bda62e10] score=0.44199562072753906 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v2.2)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Retained for reconciliation of orders placed between 2025-09-01 and 2026-03-31 only.

**A.1 Order cut-off (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 the same-day dispatch cut-off was **16:00**.

**A.2 Free shipping threshold (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 standard shipping was free on orders of **75.00 AZN or more**.

*Change record: v2.2 → v2.3 approved by Logistics on 2026-03-10, effective 2026-04-01. The cut-off was moved earlier following the change of warehouse carrier collection slot.*

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.7746782302856445 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[abde77a8-e2c8-4d0d-9414-688ec306a1b8] score=0.6743952035903931 doc=warranty-policy.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.0)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to products delivered between 2025-01-01 and 2026-01-31.

**A.1 Aurora-branded warranty (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 Aurora-branded products carried an **18-month** warranty. This was raised to 24 months in v3.1.

**A.2 Aurora Plus extension (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 there was **no** membership warranty extension.

**A.3 Diagnostic fee (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee was **15.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v3.0 → v3.1 approved by After-Sales Service on 2025-12-20, effective 2026-02-01.*

2.4 **Consumable components: 6 months.** Rechargeable batteries, filters, brush heads, printer cartridges, and lamps are covered for 6 months regardless of the product's headline warranty period and regardless of Aurora Plus membership. The 6-month consumable period is not extended by §2.3.

2.5 Where a product's headline warranty and a consumable component's warranty differ, the component follows §2.4 and the rest of the product follows §2.1 or §2.2.

6. Out-of-warranty service
6.1 Products outside the warranty period may be submitted for paid repair. Aurora Goods provides a quotation before any work begins.

6.2 The diagnostic fee is **25.00 AZN** and is waived if the customer accepts the quotation.

3. What is covered
3.1 Manufacturing defects, component failure in normal domestic use, and firmware faults that prevent the product from performing its advertised function.

3.2 Aurora Goods chooses between repair, replacement with an identical or equivalent product, and refund. The customer cannot require a specific remedy.

6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

giriş (tam)
Bağlamam Almaniyaya çatdırılıb. ORD-10018 sifarişindəki məhsulu qaytarmaq əvəzinə başqa ölçü ilə dəyişə bilərəmmi?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
Görünür, Aurora Goods-un siyasətində ölçü/model dəyişdirmə (exchange) prosesi ayrıca nəzərdə tutulmayıb — yalnız qaytarma (return) və geri ödəmə (refund) prosesi mövcuddur. Yəni birbaşa "başqa ölçü ilə dəyişmə" seçimi yoxdur; prosedur belədir:

1. Məhsulu qaytarırsınız (return),
2. Geri ödəniş alırsınız,
3. İstədiyiniz ölçünü yenidən sifariş edirsiniz.

ORD-10018 sizin Almaniyaya çatdırılan sifarişinizdir, çatdırılma tarixi 2026-08-10, bu gün isə 2026-09-01 — yəni çatdırılmadan 22 gün keçib. Beynəlxalq sifarişlər üçün qaytarma pəncərəsi **21 təqvim günüdür** (çatdırılma günü 0-cı gün sayılır). Təəssüf ki, bu müddət artıq bitib, ona görə də bu sifariş üzrə nə qaytarma, nə də dəyişdirmə mümkün deyil.

Əlavə məlumat üçün, məhsulun konkret SKU-su üzrə əsl uyğunluğu yoxlamaq istəyirsinizsə (məsələn, zədə/qüsur var — bu fərqli qaydaya tabedir), mənə bildirin.

8.2 Two consequences of this ladder are stated explicitly because they are frequently misread:
- An Aurora Plus member who buys a promotional item gets the **promotional** window, not the member window.
- An Aurora Plus member whose parcel is delivered outside Azerbaijan gets the **international** window, not the member window.

1.5 A return is a request to send goods back for a refund. A warranty claim is a request to repair or replace a defective product after the return window has closed. Warranty claims are governed by `warranty-policy.md`.

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.4775732755661011 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[836c7e8c-bec2-4e46-a640-6515116878ec] score=0.47616803646087646 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5.5 A successful price match is paid as store credit within **24 hours**, or to the original payment method within the timescales in `payments-and-billing.md` §6.2 if the customer requests it.

5.6 A price match does not make an item promotional and does not shorten its return window.

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.47236698865890503 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[41360bad-f3c0-4e2c-b858-ebbae9219738] score=0.47151362895965576 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6.4 Refunds for returned goods are approved only after warehouse inspection; see `returns-and-refunds.md` §7.2.

6.5 Where a COD order is refunded, the customer must supply an IBAN. Aurora Goods does not refund COD orders in cash.

---

[ab7a582e-fc44-4bc2-b79e-4ffc35c8988e] score=0.46826171875 doc=international-shipping.md
| Merchandise value | Terms | Who pays duties |
|---|---|---|
| 999.99 AZN | DDU | customer |
| 1,000.00 AZN | DDP | Aurora Goods |
| 1,000.01 AZN | DDP | Aurora Goods |

4.4 A parcel refused at customs, or abandoned because the customer declines to pay duties under §4.3, is treated as a refused delivery. Outbound shipping, duties already incurred, and return-to-origin freight are deducted from any refund.

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.466602623462677 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v2.2)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Retained for reconciliation of orders placed between 2025-09-01 and 2026-03-31 only.

**A.1 Order cut-off (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 the same-day dispatch cut-off was **16:00**.

**A.2 Free shipping threshold (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 standard shipping was free on orders of **75.00 AZN or more**.

*Change record: v2.2 → v2.3 approved by Logistics on 2026-03-10, effective 2026-04-01. The cut-off was moved earlier following the change of warehouse carrier collection slot.*

[c0a144df-a50a-40c8-a127-10372453edd1] score=0.48919129371643066 doc=international-shipping.md
3. Weight, size and value limits
3.1 The maximum shipping weight for a single international parcel is **30.0 kg**. A parcel weighing exactly 30.0 kg is accepted; a parcel above 30.0 kg is not accepted and the order is split or cancelled.

3.2 The maximum declared value for a single international parcel is **5,000.00 AZN**.

3.3 Cash on delivery is not available on any international order; see `payments-and-billing.md` §2.1.

---

[836c7e8c-bec2-4e46-a640-6515116878ec] score=0.4813135862350464 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5.5 A successful price match is paid as store credit within **24 hours**, or to the original payment method within the timescales in `payments-and-billing.md` §6.2 if the customer requests it.

5.6 A price match does not make an item promotional and does not shorten its return window.

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.4813106656074524 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[474ec614-20b3-46a7-b1d3-31ae8d82b22f] score=0.46857506036758423 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
7. Address changes
7.1 A delivery address can be changed free of charge until the order is dispatched.

7.2 After dispatch, an address change within the same zone costs **7.00 AZN** and is subject to carrier acceptance. Address changes across zones after dispatch are not possible.

---

[1259e653-711d-4230-bb48-72bb926a7a98] score=0.4656662940979004 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8.2 Two consequences of this ladder are stated explicitly because they are frequently misread:
- An Aurora Plus member who buys a promotional item gets the **promotional** window, not the member window.
- An Aurora Plus member whose parcel is delivered outside Azerbaijan gets the **international** window, not the member window.

---

[0820fc7e-4926-4475-81c9-44d1df7a2ba8] score=0.4656456708908081 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5. Price match
5.1 Aurora Goods matches a lower advertised price for an identical item, in stock, sold and delivered by a competitor registered in Azerbaijan.

5.2 A price match claim must be made within **14 calendar days** of the order date. Note that this period runs from the **order date**, not the delivery date, and is therefore counted differently from every return window in this corpus.

5.3 The maximum amount refunded under a single price match claim is **200.00 AZN**.

5.4 Price match is not available on:
- clearance and Final Sale items;
- items bought with a promotional code;
- marketplace, auction, second-hand, and grey-import listings;
- competitor prices that require a membership, app install, or bundle purchase to obtain;
- items where the competitor's total price including delivery is not lower than the Aurora Goods total price including delivery.

qadağan olunmuş ifadə(lər) cavabdadır: ['14-day']
giriş (tam)
My domestic parcel arrived damaged. How many days do I have to report it?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
You have **7 calendar days** from the delivery date to report a domestic parcel that arrived damaged.

- This is a separate deadline from the standard 14-day return window — it exists so carrier claims can be filed quickly.
- You'll need to include photos of both the item and the outer packaging.
- If you report it after 7 days, it's not automatically refused, but it will be handled as a warranty claim instead (and photo evidence is still required).

If your parcel is already damaged, let me know the order ID and I can check the details for you.

[1d517561-dac2-4d6d-8ff3-c47a816319c5] score=0.6108078956604004 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
5. Items damaged, defective, or missing on arrival
5.1 Damage visible on arrival, missing items from a multi-item parcel, and parcels that arrive opened must be reported within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date.

5.2 This 7-day reporting deadline is a **separate obligation** from the return window in §2. It exists because carrier claims must be filed quickly. A customer who reports damage on day 3 and a customer who initiates an ordinary change-of-mind return on day 13 are both acting within their respective deadlines; the two periods do not override one another.

5.3 Damage reported after the 7-day deadline is not refused automatically, but it is handled as a warranty claim under `warranty-policy.md` rather than as a transit damage claim, and photographic evidence is required.

5.4 Reports under this section must include photographs of the item and of the outer packaging.

---

[e2c5080f-0986-40ea-86e6-c8b9307f509b] score=0.5187736749649048 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
6. Failed delivery attempts
6.1 The carrier makes up to **3 delivery attempts** on consecutive business days.

6.2 After the third failed attempt the parcel is held at the local depot for **5 calendar days**.

6.3 If the parcel is not collected within the hold period, it is returned to the Aurora Goods warehouse. The order is then refunded minus the original outbound shipping charge; the return label fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §4.2 is not applied.

6.4 A parcel returned to the warehouse under §6.3 cannot be re-sent. The customer must place a new order.

---

[9eea4e99-d1f9-4149-9e15-b7a34cf49d52] score=0.5163598656654358 doc=international-shipping.md
6. Transit damage on international orders
6.1 The transit-damage reporting deadline for international orders is **14 calendar days** from delivery, rather than the 7 days in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1. The longer period reflects customs handling.

---

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.4739481806755066 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

[ad6af8c8-ddb1-4a8d-a41a-87eaea9439c0] score=0.5356789231300354 doc=warranty-policy.md
2.4 **Consumable components: 6 months.** Rechargeable batteries, filters, brush heads, printer cartridges, and lamps are covered for 6 months regardless of the product's headline warranty period and regardless of Aurora Plus membership. The 6-month consumable period is not extended by §2.3.

2.5 Where a product's headline warranty and a consumable component's warranty differ, the component follows §2.4 and the rest of the product follows §2.1 or §2.2.

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.5226211547851562 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[c2c04453-c31a-4b1d-a43c-7ed65db5cbe4] score=0.5166528820991516 doc=warranty-policy.md
1.5 **Counting rule.** A warranty of N months expires at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the day of the month N months after the delivery date that has the same day number as the delivery date. That final day is inside the warranty period. Where the target month has no such day number, the period ends on the last day of that month.

| Delivery date | Warranty | Last covered day |
|---|---|---|
| 2025-09-01 | 12 months | 2026-09-01 |
| 2025-08-31 | 12 months | 2026-08-31 |
| 2024-09-01 | 24 months | 2026-09-01 |
| 2026-03-31 | 6 months | 2026-09-30 |

---

[d9152578-8b9e-4632-bca3-8ec0e0722dda] score=0.500416100025177 doc=warranty-policy.md
1.3 **Which version applies.** The length of a product's warranty is fixed by the version of this policy that was in force on the **delivery date**, and does not change when this policy is later revised. A product delivered on 2025-06-10 is therefore covered for the period stated in v3.0, not the period stated in v3.1. Service charges that are incurred today — for example the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee in §6.2 — are charged at the current rate. Appendix A exists for this reason and is not decorative.

1.4 A warranty claim can be opened at any point inside the warranty period, including while the return window is still open.

[bc8552dc-6d8e-496f-b1f0-040d9f7fccb4] score=0.49700939655303955 doc=international-shipping.md
7. Warranty on international orders
7.1 The warranty periods in `warranty-policy.md` §2 apply worldwide and are not shortened for international customers.

7.2 The customer pays outbound carriage to the Aurora Goods service centre for an international warranty claim. Aurora Goods pays return carriage of the repaired or replaced item.

[abde77a8-e2c8-4d0d-9414-688ec306a1b8] score=0.48833179473876953 doc=warranty-policy.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.0)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to products delivered between 2025-01-01 and 2026-01-31.

**A.1 Aurora-branded warranty (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 Aurora-branded products carried an **18-month** warranty. This was raised to 24 months in v3.1.

**A.2 Aurora Plus extension (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 there was **no** membership warranty extension.

**A.3 Diagnostic fee (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee was **15.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v3.0 → v3.1 approved by After-Sales Service on 2025-12-20, effective 2026-02-01.*

[3a6c4734-b6d2-4859-93c3-d519503d74ba] score=0.4795182943344116 doc=warranty-policy.md
5. Making a claim
5.1 Claims require the order number and a description of the fault. Photographs or a short video are required for physical symptoms.

5.2 After-Sales Service responds to a submitted claim within **3 business days**.

5.3 If the claim is accepted, the customer receives a prepaid shipping label. Assessment at the service centre takes up to **10 business days** from receipt.

5.4 Repaired or replaced products carry the **remainder of the original warranty period, or 3 months, whichever is longer**.

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.6291472911834717 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.5910671949386597 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.5767114162445068 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[a7983410-b1e6-4e17-b7b5-5d456ebe2753] score=0.5462689995765686 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2.4 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to promotional returns unchanged, calculated on the **price actually paid**, not on the list price.

2.5 The 7-day window does not affect warranty rights. A promotional item carries the same warranty as the same item bought at full price.

2.6 The 7-day window does not affect the transit-damage reporting deadline in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1, which is also 7 days but is a separate obligation with a separate outcome.

---

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.5429728627204895 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.5261826515197754 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.5236566662788391 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.5229235291481018 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

giriş (tam)
My order total is 199.99 AZN. Can I pay in instalments?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
Under current policy, instalments require an order total of **200.00 AZN or more**. Your order at 199.99 AZN falls just short of that minimum, so it isn't eligible for instalment payment.

A couple of other conditions to note for future orders that do qualify:
- Instalment plans (3, 6, or 12 months, interest-free) are only available on cards issued by partner banks in Azerbaijan.
- They can't be combined with cash on delivery.

[04b28d45-04dd-42b0-8fba-4d1ccb2f9c2f] score=0.6419070363044739 doc=payments-and-billing.md
4. Instalments
4.1 Interest-free instalments are offered on orders of **200.00 AZN or more**, in 3, 6, or 12 monthly instalments.

4.2 Instalment plans are available only on cards issued by partner banks in Azerbaijan, and not with COD.

4.3 If an order paid by instalments is returned, the refund closes the remaining instalment schedule. The customer's bank reverses future instalments within **10 business days**.

---

5.5 A successful price match is paid as store credit within **24 hours**, or to the original payment method within the timescales in `payments-and-billing.md` §6.2 if the customer requests it.

5.6 A price match does not make an item promotional and does not shorten its return window.

[115ec2f4-ce6d-4e2a-8c98-0005002bf292] score=0.4990626573562622 doc=payments-and-billing.md
1. Accepted payment methods
1.1 Aurora Goods accepts:
- Visa and Mastercard debit and credit cards issued in Azerbaijan or abroad;
- Apple Pay and Google Pay;
- bank transfer against a proforma invoice;
- cash on delivery (COD), domestic orders only;
- Aurora Goods gift cards;
- Aurora Goods store credit.

1.2 All prices and all charges are in Azerbaijani manat (AZN). Aurora Goods does not process payments in any other currency. Cards issued abroad are charged in AZN and converted by the issuing bank at its own rate.

[e0c177c4-0b5b-408c-a863-fb1961a13f21] score=0.47340530157089233 doc=payments-and-billing.md
5. Failed and declined payments
5.1 For recurring charges (Aurora Plus renewal), a declined payment is retried up to **3 times**, at **24-hour** intervals.

5.2 After the third failed retry the subscription is suspended. See `account-and-membership.md` §6.

5.3 For one-off orders there is no automatic retry. The order is held as unpaid for **48 hours** and then cancelled, and reserved stock is released.

pattern tapıldı (tapılmamalıydı): 'lock'
giriş (tam)
I have now entered my password wrongly 4 times in a row. Is my account locked?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
I don't handle account login or password issues directly — that's managed by our account security system rather than something I can look up here.No — your account only locks after **5 consecutive failed sign-in attempts**, and locks for **30 minutes**. At 4 failed attempts you're not locked yet, but one more wrong password will lock it.

A couple of tips:
- A successful sign-in resets the failed-attempt counter to zero.
- If you're not confident of your password, use the password reset link instead of risking a 5th attempt — it's valid for 24 hours and works once.

1. Account creation and access
1.1 An account requires a valid email address and a password of at least **10 characters**.

1.2 After **5 consecutive failed sign-in attempts** the account is locked for **30 minutes**. The counter resets after a successful sign-in or after the lock expires.

1.3 A password reset link is valid for **24 hours** and can be used once.

6. Failed delivery attempts
6.1 The carrier makes up to **3 delivery attempts** on consecutive business days.

6.2 After the third failed attempt the parcel is held at the local depot for **5 calendar days**.

6.3 If the parcel is not collected within the hold period, it is returned to the Aurora Goods warehouse. The order is then refunded minus the original outbound shipping charge; the return label fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §4.2 is not applied.

6.4 A parcel returned to the warehouse under §6.3 cannot be re-sent. The customer must place a new order.

[837334e7-ed8a-4a40-9a39-074666b58916] score=0.419123113155365 doc=privacy-and-data.md
3. Erasure of personal data
3.1 A customer may request erasure of their personal data at any time.

3.2 An erasure request enters a **14-calendar-day grace period** during which the customer may cancel it by signing in. This exists so that an erasure triggered by a compromised account can be reversed.

3.3 After the grace period ends, erasure is completed within a further **30 calendar days**. Total elapsed time from request to completion is therefore up to 44 calendar days.

3.4 Erasure is irreversible once completed. Order history, store credit, and Aurora Plus membership are lost.

3.5 An erasure request cannot be completed while an order is in transit, a return is open, a warranty claim is open, or a chargeback is unresolved. In those cases the request is queued and the grace period begins when the last open item closes.

giriş (tam)
A seasonal-campaign item was marked down by 49 percent. It is not flagged as end-of-line stock. Is it treated as clearance or as an ordinary promotional item?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
This item is treated as **Clearance**, not just an ordinary promotional item.

Reason: an item is classified as Clearance whenever it's discounted **50% or more** off list price, *or* it's explicitly flagged as end-of-line stock by Commercial (policy §4.2). Since this item is end-of-line stock, that alone puts it into Clearance regardless of the 49% markdown — the discount percentage doesn't need to reach 50% in this case.

Practical effect: Clearance/Final Sale items **cannot be returned or exchanged for any reason other than a defect**, and there is no return window (§4.1). This clearance status overrides any promotional-return rules that might otherwise apply (§4.3). The item does keep its full standard warranty (§4.4).

[bc5119ba-30f4-46b8-b3e9-e5fd79830aec] score=0.5706724524497986 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
4. Clearance and Final Sale
4.1 Items marked **Clearance / Final Sale** cannot be returned or exchanged for any reason other than a defect. There is no return window.

4.2 An item is placed in Clearance when it is discounted **50% or more** off list price, or when it is explicitly flagged as end-of-line stock by Commercial.

4.3 Clearance status takes precedence over every other return rule, including the promotional window and the Aurora Plus window. This is rank 1 of the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

4.4 Clearance items keep their full warranty under `warranty-policy.md`.

[256e1f18-677c-4d33-bed8-bea197b506ea] score=0.5366082787513733 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
1. When an item counts as promotional
1.1 An item is **promotional** if any of the following is true at the moment of purchase:

- it was sold at a discount of **30% or more** off its list price;
- a promotional code was applied to it;
- it was part of a named seasonal campaign (Novruz, Back to School, Black Friday, Year End);
- it was a bundle component sold below its individual list price.

| Discount off list price | Promotional? |
|---|---|
| 29% | No — standard rules apply |
| 30% | Yes |
| 31% | Yes |

1.2 A discount of less than 30% applied without a promotional code and outside a named campaign does **not** make an item promotional. Ordinary markdowns and price reductions are not campaigns.

3. Promotional code rules
3.1 **One promotional code per order.** Codes cannot be combined with one another.

3.2 A promotional code cannot be applied after an order has been submitted, and an order cannot be cancelled and re-placed solely to apply a code.

3.3 Percentage codes apply to merchandise value only, never to shipping charges, COD handling fees, or the Aurora Plus membership fee.

giriş (tam)
When exactly do you charge my card for a pre-order, and what happens if the price drops before it ships?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
I don't see a specific "pre-order" price-protection policy in the knowledge base, but here's what does apply based on our payments rules:

**When your card is charged:**
- At order placement, the issuer only puts an **authorisation hold** for the order total — this is not a charge and won't show as a completed transaction.
- Aurora Goods **captures (actually charges) the amount at dispatch**, not when you place the order.
- If the order is cancelled before dispatch, the hold is released — typically within 7 calendar days (set by your card issuer, not us).

**If the price drops before it ships:**
The knowledge base doesn't have a rule covering automatic price adjustments for pre-orders before dispatch. The only price-related protection I can find is our **price match** policy (§5), which lets you claim a lower price from a registered Azerbaijani competitor within 14 calendar days of your *order* date, capped at 200.00 AZN — but that's for matching a competitor's price, not for our own price drops. It also excludes items bought with a promo code or on clearance/Final Sale.

Since a genuine "our price dropped before your pre-order shipped" scenario isn't addressed, I'll escalate this so a human can confirm whether any adjustment applies in your case.I've opened ticket **ESC-20260901-0001** for the part I couldn't answer from policy. Summary:

**Charging timing (confirmed):**
- At order placement: only an authorisation **hold** is placed on your card — not an actual charge.
- The card is **actually charged (captured) at dispatch**, not when you place the pre-order.
- If you cancel before dispatch, the hold is released — your issuer typically does this within 7 calendar days.

**Price drop before shipping:**
Our knowledge base doesn't have a specific rule for Aurora Goods lowering its own price on a pre-order before it ships. We do have a **price match** policy, but that only applies to matching a *competitor's* lower price (Azerbaijan-registered seller, within 14 days of order date, capped at 200 AZN, and excluded for promo-coded or clearance items) — it doesn't cover our own price changes. I've escalated your question to a human agent (ticket ESC-20260901-0001) to confirm whether any adjustment applies in your situation. They'll follow up with you directly.

Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v2.2)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Retained for reconciliation of orders placed between 2025-09-01 and 2026-03-31 only.

**A.1 Order cut-off (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 the same-day dispatch cut-off was **16:00**.

**A.2 Free shipping threshold (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 standard shipping was free on orders of **75.00 AZN or more**.

*Change record: v2.2 → v2.3 approved by Logistics on 2026-03-10, effective 2026-04-01. The cut-off was moved earlier following the change of warehouse carrier collection slot.*

[35107c0d-dcfc-47dc-9b3d-761cefbf06a8] score=0.5557840466499329 doc=payments-and-billing.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v1.8)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to orders placed between 2025-06-01 and 2026-04-30.

**A.1 COD limit (superseded 2026-05-01).** Under v1.8 cash on delivery was available for orders of **300.00 AZN or less**.

**A.2 Instalment minimum (superseded 2026-05-01).** Under v1.8 the instalment minimum was **150.00 AZN** and only 3- and 6-month plans were offered.

**A.3 Store credit bonus (superseded 2026-05-01).** Under v1.8 store credit carried **no bonus**.

*Change record: v1.8 → v1.9 approved by Finance Operations on 2026-04-08, effective 2026-05-01.*

[604bba65-a1d7-409d-9c8e-0a420bda16d3] score=0.5484668612480164 doc=payments-and-billing.md
3. Authorisation and holds
3.1 When an order is placed by card, the issuer places an authorisation hold for the order total. The order is confirmed only once authorisation succeeds; see `shipping-and-delivery.md` §2.4.

3.2 Aurora Goods captures the authorised amount at dispatch, not at order placement.

3.3 If an order is cancelled before dispatch, the authorisation is released. Issuers release holds within **7 calendar days**; Aurora Goods cannot shorten this period.

3.4 An authorisation hold is not a charge and does not appear on a card statement as a completed transaction.

---

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.5254676938056946 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

[53fdcc0d-197a-4ba3-a497-5d391578ebf4] score=0.5244184136390686 doc=payments-and-billing.md
8. Chargebacks
8.1 Where a chargeback is opened, the order is frozen and no return, refund, or warranty claim can be processed on it until the chargeback is resolved by the issuer.

8.2 Issuers typically resolve chargebacks within **45 calendar days**. Aurora Goods has no control over this period.

---

[f075e64d-ae2b-4980-8851-39f147473e4f] score=0.5188215970993042 doc=payments-and-billing.md
2. Cash on delivery
2.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, the COD handling fee, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

2.1 COD is available only for orders delivered inside Azerbaijan.

2.2 COD is available for orders with a total of **500.00 AZN or less**.

| Order total | COD available? |
|---|---|
| 499.99 AZN | Yes |
| 500.00 AZN | Yes |
| 500.01 AZN | No |

2.3 Orders above the COD limit must be paid by card, bank transfer, or wallet before dispatch.

2.4 COD orders carry a handling fee of **3.00 AZN**.

---

[474ec614-20b3-46a7-b1d3-31ae8d82b22f] score=0.5183129906654358 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
7. Address changes
7.1 A delivery address can be changed free of charge until the order is dispatched.

7.2 After dispatch, an address change within the same zone costs **7.00 AZN** and is subject to carrier acceptance. Address changes across zones after dispatch are not possible.

---

[325f5cea-5346-4a83-99ff-746960974a6d] score=0.5176317095756531 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v5.1)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to orders placed between 2026-01-01 and 2026-05-31.

**A.1 Promotional return window (superseded 2026-06-01).** Under v5.1 promotional items carried a **10-day** return window.

**A.2 Promotional threshold (superseded 2026-06-01).** Under v5.1 an item counted as promotional at a discount of **25% or more**.

**A.3 Price match cap (superseded 2026-06-01).** Under v5.1 the price match cap was **100.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v5.1 → v5.2 approved by Commercial on 2026-05-12, effective 2026-06-01.*

4.4 A parcel refused at customs, or abandoned because the customer declines to pay duties under §4.3, is treated as a refused delivery. Outbound shipping, duties already incurred, and return-to-origin freight are deducted from any refund.

Your kettle was delivered 22 days ago, and damage-on-arrival must be reported within **7 calendar days** of delivery (§5.1). Since that window has passed, this can't be handled as a transit-damage claim — instead it falls under the **warranty policy**, since it's now treated as a defective-product claim rather than a damage report.

[91f9e5e0-4b95-44a2-a81e-1b43feffefd7] score=0.5927625298500061 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1.5 A return is a request to send goods back for a refund. A warranty claim is a request to repair or replace a defective product after the return window has closed. Warranty claims are governed by `warranty-policy.md`.

---

[1d517561-dac2-4d6d-8ff3-c47a816319c5] score=0.5715510845184326 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
5. Items damaged, defective, or missing on arrival
5.1 Damage visible on arrival, missing items from a multi-item parcel, and parcels that arrive opened must be reported within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date.

5.2 This 7-day reporting deadline is a **separate obligation** from the return window in §2. It exists because carrier claims must be filed quickly. A customer who reports damage on day 3 and a customer who initiates an ordinary change-of-mind return on day 13 are both acting within their respective deadlines; the two periods do not override one another.

5.3 Damage reported after the 7-day deadline is not refused automatically, but it is handled as a warranty claim under `warranty-policy.md` rather than as a transit damage claim, and photographic evidence is required.

5.4 Reports under this section must include photographs of the item and of the outer packaging.

---

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.5633116960525513 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.5565505623817444 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[a7983410-b1e6-4e17-b7b5-5d456ebe2753] score=0.5497709512710571 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2.4 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to promotional returns unchanged, calculated on the **price actually paid**, not on the list price.

2.5 The 7-day window does not affect warranty rights. A promotional item carries the same warranty as the same item bought at full price.

2.6 The 7-day window does not affect the transit-damage reporting deadline in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1, which is also 7 days but is a separate obligation with a separate outcome.

---

[bc5119ba-30f4-46b8-b3e9-e5fd79830aec] score=0.5448404550552368 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
4. Clearance and Final Sale
4.1 Items marked **Clearance / Final Sale** cannot be returned or exchanged for any reason other than a defect. There is no return window.

4.2 An item is placed in Clearance when it is discounted **50% or more** off list price, or when it is explicitly flagged as end-of-line stock by Commercial.

4.3 Clearance status takes precedence over every other return rule, including the promotional window and the Aurora Plus window. This is rank 1 of the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

4.4 Clearance items keep their full warranty under `warranty-policy.md`.

---

[78ea07fc-bdd3-4417-af7a-ccd369fc9cd1] score=0.5377217531204224 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
6. Items that cannot be returned
6.1 The following are non-returnable in all cases:
- personal care and grooming items once the hygiene seal is broken;
- perishable goods;
- custom-made, personalised, or engraved items;
- digital downloads and software licence keys once the key has been revealed;
- items sold as **Clearance / Final Sale** (see `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4).

6.2 The exclusions in §6.1 take precedence over every other rule in this policy, including the Aurora Plus extension.

---

[522fd613-08c3-402c-b0db-b74866a8caab] score=0.5330838561058044 doc=warranty-policy.md
1. Scope and relationship to returns
1.1 A warranty claim is a request to repair or replace a product that has failed in normal use. It is not the same as a return. A customer who simply changed their mind must use `returns-and-refunds.md`; a customer whose product stopped working must use this policy.

1.2 The warranty period runs from the **delivery date**, not from the order date and not from the date the product was first used.

giriş (tam)
What is the standard return window?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
The standard return window at Aurora Goods is **14 calendar days** from the delivery date (delivery date counts as day 0, so the last day to initiate a return is day 14).

A few notes:
- This applies to items that are not clearance/non-returnable, not damaged on arrival, not promotional, and not bought by an Aurora Plus member — those cases have different windows (7, 21, or 30 days, or none at all for clearance).
- Once a return (RMA) is issued, you have 5 calendar days to hand the parcel to the carrier, or the RMA expires.

If you'd like me to check a specific order's eligibility, just give me the order ID.
