[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.6058871746063232 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[ac6b0e79-2024-4676-8c5e-a117f9f05f6c] score=0.5629748702049255 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
8. Precedence ladder
8.1 When two or more rules could apply to the same item, apply them in this order. The first matching rule decides the return window; lower rules are not applied.

| Rank | Rule | Where defined |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Non-returnable category / Clearance Final Sale | this document §6, `promotions-and-price-match.md` §4 |
| 2 | Damaged, defective, or wrong item on arrival | this document §5 |
| 3 | Delivery address outside Azerbaijan | `international-shipping.md` §5 |
| 4 | Promotional item | `promotions-and-price-match.md` §2 |
| 5 | Aurora Plus member | `account-and-membership.md` §3 |
| 6 | Standard return window (14 days) | this document §2 |

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.5557430386543274 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.5537772178649902 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.5272589325904846 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.5155980587005615 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.510813295841217 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

giriş (tam)
How many calendar days do I have to return a standard order?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
You have **14 calendar days** to return a standard order.

- The delivery date counts as day 0.
- The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after delivery.
- Weekends and holidays count toward the total.

Note: this applies only if the item isn't promotional, clearance, damaged on arrival, international, or covered by an Aurora Plus extended window — those cases have different windows (7, 21, or 30 days respectively). Let me know if any of those apply to your order and I can check the specifics.

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.6814826130867004 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.6188187599182129 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.6125803589820862 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[e590190d-e833-47b5-b572-d502523f2789] score=0.5145775675773621 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

giriş (tam)
What is the daily order cut-off time for same-business-day dispatch?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
The current cut-off for same-business-day dispatch is **14:00 (Asia/Baku time)**.

- Orders paid and confirmed at or before 14:00 on a business day ship the same business day.
- Orders confirmed after 14:00 ship the next business day.
- Orders confirmed on a weekend or public holiday ship the next business day regardless of time.

Note: this is the current rule effective 2026-04-01. An older 16:00 cut-off applied to orders placed between 2025-09-01 and 2026-03-31, if that's relevant to your order.

[349dad06-a776-4375-a52e-c004ec227397] score=0.6508316993713379 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
2. Order cut-off and dispatch
2.1 Orders that are paid and confirmed at or before **14:00** on a business day are dispatched the same business day.

2.2 Orders confirmed after 14:00 are dispatched on the next business day.

| Order confirmation time | Dispatch |
|---|---|
| 13:59 | same business day |
| 14:00 | same business day |
| 14:01 | next business day |

2.3 Business days are Monday to Friday, excluding public holidays announced by the Cabinet of Ministers. Orders confirmed on a Saturday, Sunday, or public holiday are dispatched on the next business day regardless of the time of confirmation.

2.4 An order is "confirmed" when payment authorisation succeeds, not when the order is submitted. See `payments-and-billing.md` §3 for authorisation timing.

---

[bd129aaa-6a0d-4fd8-8717-d173bda62e10] score=0.6235092878341675 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v2.2)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Retained for reconciliation of orders placed between 2025-09-01 and 2026-03-31 only.

**A.1 Order cut-off (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 the same-day dispatch cut-off was **16:00**.

**A.2 Free shipping threshold (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 standard shipping was free on orders of **75.00 AZN or more**.

*Change record: v2.2 → v2.3 approved by Logistics on 2026-03-10, effective 2026-04-01. The cut-off was moved earlier following the change of warehouse carrier collection slot.*

[762ead9a-46e4-4fc2-9f33-3716ee502458] score=0.5375957489013672 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
3. Delivery zones and service levels
3.1 Delivery estimates are counted in **business days from dispatch**, not from order placement.

| Zone | Coverage | Standard delivery | Express delivery |
|---|---|---|---|
| Zone 1 | Baku and Absheron peninsula | 1–2 business days | next business day |
| Zone 2 | Ganja, Sumgait, Mingachevir, Shirvan, Lankaran, Sheki, Nakhchivan city | 2–4 business days | 1–2 business days |
| Zone 3 | all other settlements | 4–7 business days | not available |

3.2 Delivery estimates are estimates, not guarantees. A parcel is treated as **delayed** only once it exceeds the upper bound of its zone estimate by **3 business days**.

3.3 Express delivery is available for orders confirmed before the cut-off in §2.1 and is not available to Zone 3.

---

[caaeb991-0d84-4cfd-8dc3-6b41686e41d2] score=0.5281445384025574 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
1. Scope
1.1 This document covers domestic delivery only. Deliveries outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md`, which has its own service levels, weight limits, and duty rules.

1.2 All times in this document are Asia/Baku (UTC+04:00). Azerbaijan does not observe daylight saving time, so this offset is constant.

---

[3b672faf-3dba-44e8-b910-1998c94e24d9] score=0.5178954601287842 doc=international-shipping.md
2. Destinations and zones
| Zone | Destinations | Transit time |
|---|---|---|
| Zone A | Georgia, Türkiye, Kazakhstan, Russia | 5–8 business days |
| Zone B | European Union, United Kingdom, United Arab Emirates | 8–14 business days |
| Zone C | all other served destinations | 14–21 business days |

2.1 Transit times are counted in business days from dispatch and exclude time held in customs.

2.2 Aurora Goods does not ship to destinations under Azerbaijani or international trade restriction. The current excluded list is maintained by Logistics and shown at checkout.

---

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.5160505771636963 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[92332ad4-ed1b-454e-a189-98883dd44ac2] score=0.5108625292778015 doc=international-shipping.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v1.5)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to orders placed between 2025-11-01 and 2026-06-30.

**A.1 International return window (superseded 2026-07-01).** Under v1.5 the international return window was **14 calendar days**, the same as the domestic window at that time.

**A.2 DDP threshold (superseded 2026-07-01).** Under v1.5 all international orders were shipped **DDU** with no DDP threshold.

**A.3 Parcel weight limit (superseded 2026-07-01).** Under v1.5 the maximum international parcel weight was **20.0 kg**.

*Change record: v1.5 → v1.6 approved by Logistics on 2026-06-04, effective 2026-07-01.*

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.5026819705963135 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
Ваш пылесос Aurora Home Cordless Vacuum (доставлен 01.09.2024) — это товар бренда Aurora, поэтому по политике, действовавшей на дату доставки (v3.0, актуальна для доставок в период 2025-01-01 – 2026-01-31... но ваша доставка была раньше, 2024-09-01, до вступления в силу v3.0), нужно уточнить: сама действующая версия политики (v3.1) относит продукты бренда Aurora к 24-месячной гарантии, а предыдущая версия (v3.0, действовавшая с 2025-01-01) — к 18 месяцам. Для доставки в сентябре 2024 года (до v3.0) явного указания в базе знаний нет.Ваша учётная запись никогда не имела Aurora Plus, так что вопрос про членство не влияет — но остаётся вопрос про версию политики, действовавшую в момент доставки (сентябрь 2024), поскольку база знаний явно описывает только периоды с 2025-01-01 (v3.0) и с 2026-02-01 (v3.1). Я не могу с уверенностью назвать гарантийный срок для доставки, случившейся до вступления в силу v3.0, не рискуя ошибиться, поэтому передам это человеку-специалисту, чтобы уточнить условия, применимые к вашей дате доставки.Я уточнила данные по вашему заказу:

- **Товар**: Aurora Home Cordless Vacuum (бренд Aurora), доставлен **01.09.2024**.
- **Aurora Plus**: у вас никогда не было этой подписки, поэтому продление гарантии на +6 месяцев не применяется.

Проблема в том, что база знаний Aurora Goods описывает гарантийные сроки для товаров бренда Aurora только начиная с **2025 года** (18 месяцев по версии политики v3.0, действовавшей с 01.01.2025 по 31.01.2026) и с **2026-02-01** (24 месяца по действующей версии v3.1). Ваш товар был доставлен раньше — в сентябре 2024 года, — и точный срок гарантии, применимый к этой дате, в доступных мне данных не указан.

Чтобы не дать вам неверную информацию, я передала ваш вопрос специалисту (тикет **ESC-20260901-0001**, очередь tier2_policy). Он уточнит корректный гарантийный срок для доставок 2024 года и свяжется с вами.

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.6685832738876343 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[ad6af8c8-ddb1-4a8d-a41a-87eaea9439c0] score=0.604048490524292 doc=warranty-policy.md
2.4 **Consumable components: 6 months.** Rechargeable batteries, filters, brush heads, printer cartridges, and lamps are covered for 6 months regardless of the product's headline warranty period and regardless of Aurora Plus membership. The 6-month consumable period is not extended by §2.3.

2.5 Where a product's headline warranty and a consumable component's warranty differ, the component follows §2.4 and the rest of the product follows §2.1 or §2.2.

---

[c2c04453-c31a-4b1d-a43c-7ed65db5cbe4] score=0.6025952100753784 doc=warranty-policy.md
1.5 **Counting rule.** A warranty of N months expires at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the day of the month N months after the delivery date that has the same day number as the delivery date. That final day is inside the warranty period. Where the target month has no such day number, the period ends on the last day of that month.

| Delivery date | Warranty | Last covered day |
|---|---|---|
| 2025-09-01 | 12 months | 2026-09-01 |
| 2025-08-31 | 12 months | 2026-08-31 |
| 2024-09-01 | 24 months | 2026-09-01 |
| 2026-03-31 | 6 months | 2026-09-30 |

---

[bc8552dc-6d8e-496f-b1f0-040d9f7fccb4] score=0.5564847588539124 doc=international-shipping.md
7. Warranty on international orders
7.1 The warranty periods in `warranty-policy.md` §2 apply worldwide and are not shortened for international customers.

7.2 The customer pays outbound carriage to the Aurora Goods service centre for an international warranty claim. Aurora Goods pays return carriage of the repaired or replaced item.

[3a6c4734-b6d2-4859-93c3-d519503d74ba] score=0.5556296706199646 doc=warranty-policy.md
5. Making a claim
5.1 Claims require the order number and a description of the fault. Photographs or a short video are required for physical symptoms.

5.2 After-Sales Service responds to a submitted claim within **3 business days**.

5.3 If the claim is accepted, the customer receives a prepaid shipping label. Assessment at the service centre takes up to **10 business days** from receipt.

5.4 Repaired or replaced products carry the **remainder of the original warranty period, or 3 months, whichever is longer**.

[0820fc7e-4926-4475-81c9-44d1df7a2ba8] score=0.5366453528404236 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5. Price match
5.1 Aurora Goods matches a lower advertised price for an identical item, in stock, sold and delivered by a competitor registered in Azerbaijan.

5.2 A price match claim must be made within **14 calendar days** of the order date. Note that this period runs from the **order date**, not the delivery date, and is therefore counted differently from every return window in this corpus.

5.3 The maximum amount refunded under a single price match claim is **200.00 AZN**.

5.4 Price match is not available on:
- clearance and Final Sale items;
- items bought with a promotional code;
- marketplace, auction, second-hand, and grey-import listings;
- competitor prices that require a membership, app install, or bundle purchase to obtain;
- items where the competitor's total price including delivery is not lower than the Aurora Goods total price including delivery.

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.7118268013000488 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.6497273445129395 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.64798504114151 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.6149201989173889 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.5727385878562927 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.7501795887947083 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.7121973037719727 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.6962642669677734 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[e590190d-e833-47b5-b572-d502523f2789] score=0.6451879739761353 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.6435311436653137 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.6298179030418396 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[80a799e2-838b-4882-9ad6-d6fad63bc811] score=0.6265521049499512 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
7. Refunds
7.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. Refund processing times, store credit options, and failed-refund handling are defined in `payments-and-billing.md` §6.

7.2 Aurora Goods begins refund processing after the returned goods have been received and inspected at the warehouse. Inspection is completed within **3 business days** of receipt.

7.3 Original outbound shipping charges are refunded only when the return is caused by an Aurora Goods error.

---

[1d517561-dac2-4d6d-8ff3-c47a816319c5] score=0.62437903881073 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
5. Items damaged, defective, or missing on arrival
5.1 Damage visible on arrival, missing items from a multi-item parcel, and parcels that arrive opened must be reported within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date.

5.2 This 7-day reporting deadline is a **separate obligation** from the return window in §2. It exists because carrier claims must be filed quickly. A customer who reports damage on day 3 and a customer who initiates an ordinary change-of-mind return on day 13 are both acting within their respective deadlines; the two periods do not override one another.

5.3 Damage reported after the 7-day deadline is not refused automatically, but it is handled as a warranty claim under `warranty-policy.md` rather than as a transit damage claim, and photographic evidence is required.

5.4 Reports under this section must include photographs of the item and of the outer packaging.

---

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.6394654512405396 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.6316338777542114 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[a7983410-b1e6-4e17-b7b5-5d456ebe2753] score=0.6160391569137573 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2.4 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to promotional returns unchanged, calculated on the **price actually paid**, not on the list price.

2.5 The 7-day window does not affect warranty rights. A promotional item carries the same warranty as the same item bought at full price.

2.6 The 7-day window does not affect the transit-damage reporting deadline in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1, which is also 7 days but is a separate obligation with a separate outcome.

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.6141657829284668 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.5744895935058594 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.5526906251907349 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.5451374650001526 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[e590190d-e833-47b5-b572-d502523f2789] score=0.6301636695861816 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

[41360bad-f3c0-4e2c-b858-ebbae9219738] score=0.6013551950454712 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6.4 Refunds for returned goods are approved only after warehouse inspection; see `returns-and-refunds.md` §7.2.

6.5 Where a COD order is refunded, the customer must supply an IBAN. Aurora Goods does not refund COD orders in cash.

---

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.5927311182022095 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[63883971-d763-45eb-9bf9-010206d06827] score=0.5900990962982178 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Returns and Refunds Policy
> **Document ID:** AG-POL-RET
> **Version:** 4.0
> **Effective from:** 2026-01-01
> **Supersedes:** v3.2 (in force 2024-03-01 through 2025-12-31)
> **Scope:** orders delivered to addresses inside the Republic of Azerbaijan
> **Owner:** Customer Experience
> **Last reviewed:** 2026-06-30
> **Related documents:** `promotions-and-price-match.md`, `international-shipping.md`, `warranty-policy.md`, `payments-and-billing.md`, `account-and-membership.md`

---

[7ddbd583-63fa-4a00-a73e-d9d8ec2732d0] score=0.5839806795120239 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
3. Condition of returned goods and restocking fee
3.1 Returned goods must be complete: the product, all accessories, cables, manuals, and the original manufacturer packaging.

3.2 A **restocking fee of 15%** of the price paid is deducted from the refund when the item is returned opened or unsealed but in resalable condition.

3.3 No restocking fee is charged when:
- the item is returned unopened with the manufacturer seal intact;
- the item is defective, damaged in transit, or not the item that was ordered;
- the return is the result of an Aurora Goods fulfilment error.

3.4 Items returned incomplete, with signs of use beyond reasonable inspection, or without original packaging are refused and returned to the customer at the customer's expense.

---

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.5793370008468628 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[28c6d7e0-61ff-4200-8eb7-9282993c1f34] score=0.5732765197753906 doc=international-shipping.md
5.3 Aurora Goods does **not** issue prepaid return labels for international returns. The customer arranges and pays for return carriage, except where the item is defective, damaged in transit, or incorrectly shipped, in which case Aurora Goods reimburses documented return carriage up to **120.00 AZN**.

5.4 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies unchanged.

5.5 Duties and import taxes already paid are not refunded by Aurora Goods. Recovery of duties is a matter between the customer and the destination customs authority.

5.6 The returned parcel must reach the Aurora Goods warehouse within **30 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. Parcels arriving after that are refused.

---

[80a799e2-838b-4882-9ad6-d6fad63bc811] score=0.5716805458068848 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
7. Refunds
7.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. Refund processing times, store credit options, and failed-refund handling are defined in `payments-and-billing.md` §6.

7.2 Aurora Goods begins refund processing after the returned goods have been received and inspected at the warehouse. Inspection is completed within **3 business days** of receipt.

7.3 Original outbound shipping charges are refunded only when the return is caused by an Aurora Goods error.

---

giriş (tam)
Do Aurora Goods gift cards expire, and can I get the balance back as a refund?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
Here's what applies to Aurora Goods gift cards / store credit:

- **No expiration:** Store credit does not expire.
- **Refund back to card/cash:** No — refunds are only issued to the *original payment method* used for the purchase. Store credit cannot be redirected back to a card, bank account, or another person's account.

If you'd like, I can also clarify how store credit is used or check your current balance if you give me your account email.

[e590190d-e833-47b5-b572-d502523f2789] score=0.6193698644638062 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.6031243801116943 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.594658613204956 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[2031f6ab-6c4f-4f9b-b5aa-8ee8b75a8d89] score=0.5781173706054688 doc=account-and-membership.md
5. Cancelling Aurora Plus
5.1 A membership cancelled within **14 calendar days** of the charge date is refunded in full, provided no member benefit has been used in that period. Placing an order that used free member shipping counts as using a benefit.

5.2 A membership cancelled after the 14-day period is refunded **pro rata for each whole unused month remaining**, calculated from the cancellation date. Partial months are not refunded.

5.3 Benefits end on the cancellation date, not at the end of the paid year. A warranty extension already earned under §4.2 is not withdrawn.

5.4 A membership cancelled and re-taken within **12 months** is not eligible for a further free trial.

---

[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.576763391494751 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[0820fc7e-4926-4475-81c9-44d1df7a2ba8] score=0.5753360390663147 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
5. Price match
5.1 Aurora Goods matches a lower advertised price for an identical item, in stock, sold and delivered by a competitor registered in Azerbaijan.

5.2 A price match claim must be made within **14 calendar days** of the order date. Note that this period runs from the **order date**, not the delivery date, and is therefore counted differently from every return window in this corpus.

5.3 The maximum amount refunded under a single price match claim is **200.00 AZN**.

5.4 Price match is not available on:
- clearance and Final Sale items;
- items bought with a promotional code;
- marketplace, auction, second-hand, and grey-import listings;
- competitor prices that require a membership, app install, or bundle purchase to obtain;
- items where the competitor's total price including delivery is not lower than the Aurora Goods total price including delivery.

[7ddbd583-63fa-4a00-a73e-d9d8ec2732d0] score=0.5674979090690613 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
3. Condition of returned goods and restocking fee
3.1 Returned goods must be complete: the product, all accessories, cables, manuals, and the original manufacturer packaging.

3.2 A **restocking fee of 15%** of the price paid is deducted from the refund when the item is returned opened or unsealed but in resalable condition.

3.3 No restocking fee is charged when:
- the item is returned unopened with the manufacturer seal intact;
- the item is defective, damaged in transit, or not the item that was ordered;
- the return is the result of an Aurora Goods fulfilment error.

3.4 Items returned incomplete, with signs of use beyond reasonable inspection, or without original packaging are refused and returned to the customer at the customer's expense.

---

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.5652346014976501 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

[8fe856c0-891b-47b6-b9b3-748fff04a718] score=0.5417912006378174 doc=account-and-membership.md
2. Aurora Plus membership
2.1 Aurora Plus is an annual paid membership costing **49.00 AZN per year**, charged in advance.

2.2 New members receive a **30-day free trial**. The membership fee is charged on the day after the trial ends unless the trial is cancelled before then.

2.3 One membership covers one account. Membership benefits cannot be shared with other accounts or transferred to another person.

2.4 Membership is "active" for the purpose of any benefit in this document if it was active on the date the relevant order was placed.

[e92fe2eb-d4c9-4471-bbcc-ccde33bbf249] score=0.5396847724914551 doc=account-and-membership.md

4.1 Free standard domestic shipping with **no minimum order value**, replacing the 100.00 AZN threshold in `shipping-and-delivery.md` §4.1. Express delivery charges still apply.

4.2 A **6-month warranty extension** on purchases made while the membership is active; see `warranty-policy.md` §2.3.

4.3 Early access to seasonal campaigns, normally **24 hours** before general release.

4.4 Membership benefits do not include any additional discount on list prices.

[f075e64d-ae2b-4980-8851-39f147473e4f] score=0.5125815868377686 doc=payments-and-billing.md
2. Cash on delivery
2.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, the COD handling fee, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

2.1 COD is available only for orders delivered inside Azerbaijan.

2.2 COD is available for orders with a total of **500.00 AZN or less**.

| Order total | COD available? |
|---|---|
| 499.99 AZN | Yes |
| 500.00 AZN | Yes |
| 500.01 AZN | No |

2.3 Orders above the COD limit must be paid by card, bank transfer, or wallet before dispatch.

2.4 COD orders carry a handling fee of **3.00 AZN**.

[04b28d45-04dd-42b0-8fba-4d1ccb2f9c2f] score=0.5101492404937744 doc=payments-and-billing.md
4. Instalments
4.1 Interest-free instalments are offered on orders of **200.00 AZN or more**, in 3, 6, or 12 monthly instalments.

4.2 Instalment plans are available only on cards issued by partner banks in Azerbaijan, and not with COD.

4.3 If an order paid by instalments is returned, the refund closes the remaining instalment schedule. The customer's bank reverses future instalments within **10 business days**.

5. Signature and high-value orders
5.1 Orders with a paid total **above 750.00 AZN** require an adult signature on delivery and cannot be left with a neighbour or at an unattended address.

5.2 Orders at or below 750.00 AZN may be left at the address without signature if the customer has enabled that option in their account.

|---|---|---|

4.4 A parcel refused at customs, or abandoned because the customer declines to pay duties under §4.3, is treated as a refused delivery. Outbound shipping, duties already incurred, and return-to-origin freight are deducted from any refund.

[667ed88a-1b9d-4f86-a0a2-097083a2147a] score=0.47734224796295166 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
4. Return shipping costs
4.0 **How order value is measured.** The threshold in §4.1 is measured on **merchandise value**: the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges and handling fees.

4.1 Orders with a merchandise value of **150.00 AZN or more** receive a free prepaid return label.

4.2 Orders with a merchandise value **below 150.00 AZN** are charged a return label fee of **9.90 AZN**, deducted from the refund.

4.3 Items with a shipping weight **above 20.0 kg** cannot be returned by parcel carrier. Aurora Goods arranges freight collection and deducts a collection fee of **45.00 AZN**. Items weighing exactly 20.0 kg are handled as normal parcels.

4.4 Return shipping is always free, and no restocking fee is charged, when the item is defective, damaged in transit, or incorrectly shipped.

---

[7ddbd583-63fa-4a00-a73e-d9d8ec2732d0] score=0.47070008516311646 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
3. Condition of returned goods and restocking fee
3.1 Returned goods must be complete: the product, all accessories, cables, manuals, and the original manufacturer packaging.

3.2 A **restocking fee of 15%** of the price paid is deducted from the refund when the item is returned opened or unsealed but in resalable condition.

3.3 No restocking fee is charged when:
- the item is returned unopened with the manufacturer seal intact;
- the item is defective, damaged in transit, or not the item that was ordered;
- the return is the result of an Aurora Goods fulfilment error.

3.4 Items returned incomplete, with signs of use beyond reasonable inspection, or without original packaging are refused and returned to the customer at the customer's expense.

---

retrieval (8 chunk)
[1d517561-dac2-4d6d-8ff3-c47a816319c5] score=0.5442472696304321 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
5. Items damaged, defective, or missing on arrival
5.1 Damage visible on arrival, missing items from a multi-item parcel, and parcels that arrive opened must be reported within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date.

5.2 This 7-day reporting deadline is a **separate obligation** from the return window in §2. It exists because carrier claims must be filed quickly. A customer who reports damage on day 3 and a customer who initiates an ordinary change-of-mind return on day 13 are both acting within their respective deadlines; the two periods do not override one another.

5.3 Damage reported after the 7-day deadline is not refused automatically, but it is handled as a warranty claim under `warranty-policy.md` rather than as a transit damage claim, and photographic evidence is required.

5.4 Reports under this section must include photographs of the item and of the outer packaging.

---

[9eea4e99-d1f9-4149-9e15-b7a34cf49d52] score=0.5253186225891113 doc=international-shipping.md
6. Transit damage on international orders
6.1 The transit-damage reporting deadline for international orders is **14 calendar days** from delivery, rather than the 7 days in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1. The longer period reflects customs handling.

---

[a7983410-b1e6-4e17-b7b5-5d456ebe2753] score=0.4614877700805664 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2.4 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to promotional returns unchanged, calculated on the **price actually paid**, not on the list price.

2.5 The 7-day window does not affect warranty rights. A promotional item carries the same warranty as the same item bought at full price.

2.6 The 7-day window does not affect the transit-damage reporting deadline in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1, which is also 7 days but is a separate obligation with a separate outcome.

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.4510570168495178 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

[91f9e5e0-4b95-44a2-a81e-1b43feffefd7] score=0.4431746006011963 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1.5 A return is a request to send goods back for a refund. A warranty claim is a request to repair or replace a defective product after the return window has closed. Warranty claims are governed by `warranty-policy.md`.

[3a6c4734-b6d2-4859-93c3-d519503d74ba] score=0.4349250793457031 doc=warranty-policy.md
5. Making a claim
5.1 Claims require the order number and a description of the fault. Photographs or a short video are required for physical symptoms.

5.2 After-Sales Service responds to a submitted claim within **3 business days**.

5.3 If the claim is accepted, the customer receives a prepaid shipping label. Assessment at the service centre takes up to **10 business days** from receipt.

5.4 Repaired or replaced products carry the **remainder of the original warranty period, or 3 months, whichever is longer**.

[d9152578-8b9e-4632-bca3-8ec0e0722dda] score=0.42949092388153076 doc=warranty-policy.md
1.3 **Which version applies.** The length of a product's warranty is fixed by the version of this policy that was in force on the **delivery date**, and does not change when this policy is later revised. A product delivered on 2025-06-10 is therefore covered for the period stated in v3.0, not the period stated in v3.1. Service charges that are incurred today — for example the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee in §6.2 — are charged at the current rate. Appendix A exists for this reason and is not decorative.

1.4 A warranty claim can be opened at any point inside the warranty period, including while the return window is still open.

1. Scope and relationship to returns
1.1 A warranty claim is a request to repair or replace a product that has failed in normal use. It is not the same as a return. A customer who simply changed their mind must use `returns-and-refunds.md`; a customer whose product stopped working must use this policy.

1.2 The warranty period runs from the **delivery date**, not from the order date and not from the date the product was first used.

retrieval (8 chunk)
[0368c502-5fe1-4a6a-bd9d-e3aab08fb42d] score=0.7423579096794128 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
2. Standard return window
2.1 The standard return window is **14 calendar days**.

2.2 **Counting rule.** The delivery date counts as day 0. The window closes at 23:59 (Asia/Baku) on the 14th calendar day after the delivery date. Weekends and public holidays are included in the count.

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within standard window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 13 | Yes |
| 14 | Yes — final day |
| 15 or more | No |

2.3 Once a return has been initiated, the customer must hand the parcel to the carrier within **5 calendar days** of the RMA being issued. RMAs that are not used within 5 calendar days expire and the return is cancelled.

2.4 The standard return window applies to items that are not promotional, not clearance, not damaged on arrival, and not purchased by an Aurora Plus member. Where more than one rule could apply, use the precedence ladder in §8.

---

[ee687baf-c34e-4fbc-926f-e1e7ddc6ddca] score=0.709053635597229 doc=international-shipping.md
5. International returns
5.1 The return window for international orders is **21 calendar days** from the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within international window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 20 | Yes |
| 21 | Yes — final day |
| 22 or more | No |

5.2 The 21-day international window replaces the domestic 14-day window, the 30-day Aurora Plus window, and — for international deliveries only — is applied ahead of the 7-day promotional window under rank 3 of the precedence ladder. Clearance and non-returnable categories still take precedence over it.

[78d993d1-cc62-4224-bf45-2fd0f21f3ae5] score=0.6813103556632996 doc=promotions-and-price-match.md
2. Return window for promotional items
2.1 Promotional items may be returned within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within promotional window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 6 | Yes |
| 7 | Yes — final day |
| 8 or more | No |

2.2 This is not an exception carved out of the standard 14-day window; it is a separate window that replaces it. The 14-day window in `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.1 does not apply to promotional items at all.

2.3 The 7-day promotional window also replaces the 30-day Aurora Plus window. An Aurora Plus member returning a promotional item has 7 days. This follows from the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8.

[4b84b28b-c77d-41b3-941e-800a868ef7e5] score=0.6431881189346313 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
1. Scope and definitions
1.1 This policy governs the return of physical goods purchased from Aurora Goods and delivered to a domestic address. Orders delivered outside Azerbaijan are governed by `international-shipping.md` §5.

1.2 **"Delivery date"** means the date recorded by the carrier as the date the parcel was handed to the customer or to a person at the delivery address. This date is shown in the customer's order history and is the only date used to calculate return windows.

1.3 **"Return window"** means the period during which a customer may *initiate* a return. Initiating a return means submitting a return request through the Aurora Goods website, app, or support channel and receiving a Return Merchandise Authorisation (RMA) number.

1.4 **"List price"** means the price at which the item was offered immediately before any discount, promotion, or campaign was applied.

[e590190d-e833-47b5-b572-d502523f2789] score=0.6257953643798828 doc=payments-and-billing.md
6. Refunds
6.1 Refunds are issued to the original payment method. A refund cannot be redirected to a different card or a different person's account.

6.2 Once a refund is approved, Aurora Goods submits it to the payment provider within **1 business day**. The funds then reach the customer according to the method:

| Original payment method | Time to reach customer after submission |
|---|---|
| Card (Visa / Mastercard) | 5–10 business days |
| Apple Pay / Google Pay | 5–10 business days |
| Bank transfer | 3–5 business days |
| Cash on delivery | 3–5 business days, to a bank account provided by the customer |
| Store credit | within 24 hours |

6.3 A customer may choose to receive a refund as **store credit** instead of to the original method. Store credit is issued within 24 hours and carries a **5% bonus** on the refunded amount. Store credit does not expire.

[e2c5080f-0986-40ea-86e6-c8b9307f509b] score=0.6207643747329712 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
6. Failed delivery attempts
6.1 The carrier makes up to **3 delivery attempts** on consecutive business days.

6.2 After the third failed attempt the parcel is held at the local depot for **5 calendar days**.

6.3 If the parcel is not collected within the hold period, it is returned to the Aurora Goods warehouse. The order is then refunded minus the original outbound shipping charge; the return label fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §4.2 is not applied.

6.4 A parcel returned to the warehouse under §6.3 cannot be re-sent. The customer must place a new order.

---

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.6184462904930115 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[f4293979-999f-4a6b-87d4-c8e34c163269] score=0.6111530065536499 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.7867283821105957 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[abde77a8-e2c8-4d0d-9414-688ec306a1b8] score=0.6688424348831177 doc=warranty-policy.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.0)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to products delivered between 2025-01-01 and 2026-01-31.

**A.1 Aurora-branded warranty (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 Aurora-branded products carried an **18-month** warranty. This was raised to 24 months in v3.1.

**A.2 Aurora Plus extension (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 there was **no** membership warranty extension.

**A.3 Diagnostic fee (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee was **15.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v3.0 → v3.1 approved by After-Sales Service on 2025-12-20, effective 2026-02-01.*

[ad6af8c8-ddb1-4a8d-a41a-87eaea9439c0] score=0.6665557026863098 doc=warranty-policy.md
2.4 **Consumable components: 6 months.** Rechargeable batteries, filters, brush heads, printer cartridges, and lamps are covered for 6 months regardless of the product's headline warranty period and regardless of Aurora Plus membership. The 6-month consumable period is not extended by §2.3.

2.5 Where a product's headline warranty and a consumable component's warranty differ, the component follows §2.4 and the rest of the product follows §2.1 or §2.2.

---

[86bb0505-ac79-4954-a394-77202a75a81b] score=0.6331322193145752 doc=warranty-policy.md
6. Out-of-warranty service
6.1 Products outside the warranty period may be submitted for paid repair. Aurora Goods provides a quotation before any work begins.

6.2 The diagnostic fee is **25.00 AZN** and is waived if the customer accepts the quotation.

[9ff84845-b630-4bbd-b4be-084901c78375] score=0.8119584918022156 doc=warranty-policy.md
2. Warranty periods
2.1 **Standard warranty: 12 months.** This applies to all third-party branded products sold by Aurora Goods.

2.2 **Aurora-branded products: 24 months.** Products sold under the Aurora, Aurora Home, or Aurora Studio brands carry a 24-month warranty.

2.3 **Aurora Plus extension: +6 months.** An active Aurora Plus membership at the time of purchase adds 6 months to whichever of §2.1 or §2.2 applies. The extension is tied to the purchase, not to the membership: cancelling Aurora Plus later does not remove an extension already earned.

| Product | No membership | Aurora Plus at purchase |
|---|---|---|
| Third-party brand | 12 months | 18 months |
| Aurora brand | 24 months | 30 months |

[8fe856c0-891b-47b6-b9b3-748fff04a718] score=0.7114294767379761 doc=account-and-membership.md
2. Aurora Plus membership
2.1 Aurora Plus is an annual paid membership costing **49.00 AZN per year**, charged in advance.

2.2 New members receive a **30-day free trial**. The membership fee is charged on the day after the trial ends unless the trial is cancelled before then.

2.3 One membership covers one account. Membership benefits cannot be shared with other accounts or transferred to another person.

2.4 Membership is "active" for the purpose of any benefit in this document if it was active on the date the relevant order was placed.

---

[abde77a8-e2c8-4d0d-9414-688ec306a1b8] score=0.6921814680099487 doc=warranty-policy.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.0)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Applies only to products delivered between 2025-01-01 and 2026-01-31.

**A.1 Aurora-branded warranty (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 Aurora-branded products carried an **18-month** warranty. This was raised to 24 months in v3.1.

**A.2 Aurora Plus extension (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 there was **no** membership warranty extension.

**A.3 Diagnostic fee (superseded 2026-02-01).** Under v3.0 the out-of-warranty diagnostic fee was **15.00 AZN**.

*Change record: v3.0 → v3.1 approved by After-Sales Service on 2025-12-20, effective 2026-02-01.*

[ad6af8c8-ddb1-4a8d-a41a-87eaea9439c0] score=0.6881412267684937 doc=warranty-policy.md
2.4 **Consumable components: 6 months.** Rechargeable batteries, filters, brush heads, printer cartridges, and lamps are covered for 6 months regardless of the product's headline warranty period and regardless of Aurora Plus membership. The 6-month consumable period is not extended by §2.3.

2.5 Where a product's headline warranty and a consumable component's warranty differ, the component follows §2.4 and the rest of the product follows §2.1 or §2.2.

---

[9acdf88f-87b4-4047-81ff-88769f3af845] score=0.683021605014801 doc=account-and-membership.md
3. Member return window
3.1 Aurora Plus members have an extended return window of **30 calendar days**, counted using the same rule as `returns-and-refunds.md` §2.2 (delivery date is day 0).

| Calendar days elapsed since delivery | Within member window? |
|---|---|
| 0 – 29 | Yes |
| 30 | Yes — final day |
| 31 or more | No |

3.2 The member window applies only where the precedence ladder in `returns-and-refunds.md` §8 reaches rank 5. In particular it does **not** apply to promotional items, to clearance items, to non-returnable categories, or to orders delivered outside Azerbaijan.

3.3 The restocking fee in `returns-and-refunds.md` §3.2 applies to members on the same terms as to non-members. Membership extends the window; it does not waive the fee.

---

[2031f6ab-6c4f-4f9b-b5aa-8ee8b75a8d89] score=0.6669389009475708 doc=account-and-membership.md
5. Cancelling Aurora Plus
5.1 A membership cancelled within **14 calendar days** of the charge date is refunded in full, provided no member benefit has been used in that period. Placing an order that used free member shipping counts as using a benefit.

5.2 A membership cancelled after the 14-day period is refunded **pro rata for each whole unused month remaining**, calculated from the cancellation date. Partial months are not refunded.

5.3 Benefits end on the cancellation date, not at the end of the paid year. A warranty extension already earned under §4.2 is not withdrawn.

5.4 A membership cancelled and re-taken within **12 months** is not eligible for a further free trial.

---

[9bf37918-619f-4d4f-b1d1-b3fcbaad8fbd] score=0.6484208703041077 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
4.5 Aurora Plus members receive free standard shipping with no minimum order value. See `account-and-membership.md` §4.

- **Damage reporting deadline:** 7 calendar days from delivery for domestic transit-damage claims (Azerbaijan is the destination, so the 14-day international window doesn't apply). That deadline was 2026-08-30, and your report was filed today, 2026-09-01 — **after the deadline**.

- Your claim isn't refused automatically, but it will be handled as a **warranty claim** rather than a transit-damage claim (per policy §5.3).
- You'll need to provide **photographs of the item** (the cracked earcup) as evidence.
- Under the warranty process: I'll need your claim details, After-Sales Service responds within **3 business days**, and if accepted you get a prepaid shipping label, with assessment taking up to **10 business days** from receipt at the service centre.
- No refund or store-credit amount is decided at this stage — that depends on the warranty assessment.

Can you send photos of the cracked earcup and the outer packaging? Once I have those I can log the warranty claim for you.
