[9eea4e99-d1f9-4149-9e15-b7a34cf49d52] score=0.6190455555915833 doc=international-shipping.md
6. Transit damage on international orders
6.1 The transit-damage reporting deadline for international orders is **14 calendar days** from delivery, rather than the 7 days in `returns-and-refunds.md` §5.1. The longer period reflects customs handling.

---

[1d517561-dac2-4d6d-8ff3-c47a816319c5] score=0.5818378329277039 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
5. Items damaged, defective, or missing on arrival
5.1 Damage visible on arrival, missing items from a multi-item parcel, and parcels that arrive opened must be reported within **7 calendar days** of the delivery date.

5.2 This 7-day reporting deadline is a **separate obligation** from the return window in §2. It exists because carrier claims must be filed quickly. A customer who reports damage on day 3 and a customer who initiates an ordinary change-of-mind return on day 13 are both acting within their respective deadlines; the two periods do not override one another.

5.3 Damage reported after the 7-day deadline is not refused automatically, but it is handled as a warranty claim under `warranty-policy.md` rather than as a transit damage claim, and photographic evidence is required.

5.4 Reports under this section must include photographs of the item and of the outer packaging.

---

[3a6c4734-b6d2-4859-93c3-d519503d74ba] score=0.492512583732605 doc=warranty-policy.md
5. Making a claim
5.1 Claims require the order number and a description of the fault. Photographs or a short video are required for physical symptoms.

5.2 After-Sales Service responds to a submitted claim within **3 business days**.

5.3 If the claim is accepted, the customer receives a prepaid shipping label. Assessment at the service centre takes up to **10 business days** from receipt.

5.4 Repaired or replaced products carry the **remainder of the original warranty period, or 3 months, whichever is longer**.

---

Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v3.2)
> ⚠️ **The provisions in this appendix are no longer in force.** They are retained because they still govern orders **placed on or before 2025-12-31**, which remain within the statutory record-keeping period. Do not quote this appendix for any order placed on or after 2026-01-01.

**A.1 Return window (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the standard return window was **30 calendar days** from the delivery date.

**A.2 Restocking fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 the restocking fee for opened but resalable goods was **20%** of the price paid.

**A.3 Transit damage reporting (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 transit damage had to be reported within **14 calendar days** of delivery.

**A.4 Return label fee (superseded 2026-01-01).** Under v3.2 return labels were free on all orders regardless of order value.

giriş (tam)
My domestic basket has a merchandise value of 100 AZN. Do I pay for standard shipping or is it free?
agent cavabı (yekun, tam)
Your standard domestic shipping is **free** — orders with a merchandise value of 100.00 AZN or more qualify, and your basket is exactly at that threshold. Below 100.00 AZN, a flat 5.90 AZN fee would apply.

[510e0432-e9e3-43d3-8a33-91eb4650bd19] score=0.6513820886611938 doc=international-shipping.md
4. Duties, taxes and shipping charges
4.0 **How order value is measured.** The threshold in §4.2 is measured on **merchandise value**, excluding international shipping charges and any duties.

4.1 International shipping is charged at checkout and is **never free**. There is no international equivalent of the domestic 100.00 AZN free-shipping threshold, and Aurora Plus membership does not provide free international shipping.

4.2 Orders with a merchandise value of **1,000.00 AZN or more** are shipped **DDP**: Aurora Goods prepays destination duties and import taxes, and the customer pays nothing further on delivery.

4.3 Orders below 1,000.00 AZN are shipped **DDU**: the customer is responsible for destination duties, import taxes, and any customs broker fee. These are charged by the destination authority, not by Aurora Goods.

[73cb1de6-76b1-4f0e-869f-53cb1f24fad0] score=0.6463168859481812 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
4. Shipping charges
4.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, express surcharges, heavy-item surcharges, COD handling fees, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

4.1 Standard shipping is **free** on orders with a paid total of **100.00 AZN or more**.

4.2 Orders with a paid total **below 100.00 AZN** are charged a flat standard shipping fee of **5.90 AZN**.

4.3 Express delivery costs **12.00 AZN** in Zone 1 and **19.00 AZN** in Zone 2, and this charge applies regardless of order value. The free shipping threshold in §4.1 applies only to standard shipping.

4.4 Parcels with a shipping weight **above 30.0 kg** carry a heavy-item surcharge of **25.00 AZN**. Parcels weighing exactly 30.0 kg are not surcharged.

[667ed88a-1b9d-4f86-a0a2-097083a2147a] score=0.6117150783538818 doc=returns-and-refunds.md
4. Return shipping costs
4.0 **How order value is measured.** The threshold in §4.1 is measured on **merchandise value**: the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges and handling fees.

4.1 Orders with a merchandise value of **150.00 AZN or more** receive a free prepaid return label.

4.2 Orders with a merchandise value **below 150.00 AZN** are charged a return label fee of **9.90 AZN**, deducted from the refund.

4.3 Items with a shipping weight **above 20.0 kg** cannot be returned by parcel carrier. Aurora Goods arranges freight collection and deducts a collection fee of **45.00 AZN**. Items weighing exactly 20.0 kg are handled as normal parcels.

4.4 Return shipping is always free, and no restocking fee is charged, when the item is defective, damaged in transit, or incorrectly shipped.

---

[e92fe2eb-d4c9-4471-bbcc-ccde33bbf249] score=0.583514928817749 doc=account-and-membership.md
4. Other membership benefits
4.1 Free standard domestic shipping with **no minimum order value**, replacing the 100.00 AZN threshold in `shipping-and-delivery.md` §4.1. Express delivery charges still apply.

4.2 A **6-month warranty extension** on purchases made while the membership is active; see `warranty-policy.md` §2.3.

4.3 Early access to seasonal campaigns, normally **24 hours** before general release.

4.4 Membership benefits do not include any additional discount on list prices.

---

[f075e64d-ae2b-4980-8851-39f147473e4f] score=0.5800971388816833 doc=payments-and-billing.md
2. Cash on delivery
2.0 **How order value is measured.** Every value threshold in this document is measured on **merchandise value**: the sum of the prices actually paid for the goods, excluding shipping charges, the COD handling fee, and the Aurora Plus membership fee.

2.1 COD is available only for orders delivered inside Azerbaijan.

2.2 COD is available for orders with a total of **500.00 AZN or less**.

| Order total | COD available? |
|---|---|
| 499.99 AZN | Yes |
| 500.00 AZN | Yes |
| 500.01 AZN | No |

2.3 Orders above the COD limit must be paid by card, bank transfer, or wallet before dispatch.

2.4 COD orders carry a handling fee of **3.00 AZN**.

---

[bd129aaa-6a0d-4fd8-8717-d173bda62e10] score=0.5772112607955933 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
Appendix A — Superseded provisions (v2.2)
> ⚠️ **No longer in force.** Retained for reconciliation of orders placed between 2025-09-01 and 2026-03-31 only.

**A.1 Order cut-off (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 the same-day dispatch cut-off was **16:00**.

**A.2 Free shipping threshold (superseded 2026-04-01).** Under v2.2 standard shipping was free on orders of **75.00 AZN or more**.

*Change record: v2.2 → v2.3 approved by Logistics on 2026-03-10, effective 2026-04-01. The cut-off was moved earlier following the change of warehouse carrier collection slot.*

[9bf37918-619f-4d4f-b1d1-b3fcbaad8fbd] score=0.5701406002044678 doc=shipping-and-delivery.md
4.5 Aurora Plus members receive free standard shipping with no minimum order value. See `account-and-membership.md` §4.

[ab7a582e-fc44-4bc2-b79e-4ffc35c8988e] score=0.5491094589233398 doc=international-shipping.md
| Merchandise value | Terms | Who pays duties |
|---|---|---|
| 999.99 AZN | DDU | customer |
| 1,000.00 AZN | DDP | Aurora Goods |
| 1,000.01 AZN | DDP | Aurora Goods |

4.4 A parcel refused at customs, or abandoned because the customer declines to pay duties under §4.3, is treated as a refused delivery. Outbound shipping, duties already incurred, and return-to-origin freight are deducted from any refund.
